        calls = {"n": 0}

        _yaml_safe._cached_yaml_safe_load_text.cache_clear()
        with (
            patch("sase.config.core.CONFIG_DIR", global_dir),

E                                                              'releases\n'
E                                                              '\n'
E                                                              'Runs every five minutes. '

E                                                              'approves or rejects.\n'

E                                                                      'pr_submitted_checks '

E                                                                      'comment_checks '
E                                                                      'launch the '
E                                                                      'remote checks.',

E                                                       'script': 'sase_chop_pending_checks_poll'},
E                                                      {'description': 'Mark comment '
E                                                                      'threads older '
E                                                                      'than '
E                                                                      'zombie_timeout '
E                                                                      'as ZOMBIE\n'
E                                                                      '\n'
E                                                                      'Examines '
E                                                                      'comment-entry '
E                                                                      'suffix '
E                                                                      'timestamps on '

E                                                                      'and writes a '
E                                                                      'ZOMBIE suffix '
E                                                                      'when an entry\n'
E                                                                      'exceeds '

E                                                                      'It performs no '
E                                                                      'remote comment '
E                                                                      'fetch; '

E                                                                      'starts those '
E                                                                      'checks and\n'
E                                                                      'pending_checks_poll '
E                                                                      'applies their '

E                                                       'name': 'comment_zombie_checks',
E                                                       'script': 'sase_chop_comment_zombie_checks'},
E                                                      {'description': 'Strip stale '
E                                                                      'suffixes from '

E                                                                      'mail-readiness '
E                                                                      'markers\n'
E                                                                      '\n'
E                                                                      'Normalizes '

E                                                                      'attention '
E                                                                      'markers on '
E                                                                      'terminal '
E                                                                      'statuses. It '
E                                                                      'only\n'
E                                                                      'repairs stored '
E                                                                      'suffix state and '
E                                                                      'never launches '
E                                                                      'hooks or agents.',
E                                                       'name': 'suffix_transforms',
E                                                       'script': 'sase_chop_suffix_transforms'},
E                                                      {'description': 'Release '
E                                                                      'workspace claims '

E                                                                      '\n'
E                                                                      'Reads all '

E                                                                      'workspace '
E                                                                      'claims, '
E                                                                      'regardless of '

E                                                                      'pinned '

E                                                       'script': 'sase_chop_orphan_cleanup'},

E                                                                      'claims within '
E                                                                      'seconds.',
E                                                       'name': 'stale_running_cleanup',
E                                                       'script': 'sase_chop_stale_running_cleanup'}],
E                                            'description': 'Fast lane that advances '
E                                                           'hook, mentor, and workflow '
E                                                           'lifecycle state every few '
E                                                           'seconds\n'
E                                                           '\n'
E                                                           'Runs every five seconds '
E                                                           'with a 90-second per-chop '
E                                                           'timeout so completed work '
E                                                           'is noticed and new work '
E                                                           'starts\n'
E                                                           'promptly. Put '
E                                                           'latency-sensitive Patch '
E                                                           'lifecycle reconciliation '
E                                                           'here; slower remote '
E                                                           'polling, wait\n'
E                                                           'coordination, and '
E                                                           'maintenance belong in the '
E                                                           'other lanes.',
E                                            'interval': 5},
E                                  'housekeeping': {'chops': [{'description': 'Send a '
E                                                                             'notification '
E                                                                             'digest of '

E                                                                             'hour\n'
E                                                                             '\n'

E                                                                             'log and '
E                                                                             'the last '
E                                                                             'successful '
E                                                                             'digest '

E                                                                             'notifies '
E                                                                             'only '
E                                                                             'about '
E                                                                             'newer '
E                                                                             'errors\n'
E                                                                             'within '
E                                                                             'the '
E                                                                             'rolling '
E                                                                             'one-hour '
E                                                                             'window. '
E                                                                             'The '

E                                                                             'advances '
E                                                                             'to the '
E                                                                             'newest '
E                                                                             'notified '
E                                                                             'timestamp, '
E                                                                             'preventing\n'
E                                                                             'duplicate '
E                                                                             'digests '
E                                                                             'while '
E                                                                             'leaving '
E                                                                             'unsent '
E                                                                             'errors '
E                                                                             'eligible '
E                                                                             'after a '
E                                                                             'notification '
E                                                                             'failure.',
E                                                              'name': 'error_digest',
E                                                              'script': 'sase_chop_error_digest'},
E                                                             {'description': 'Prune '
E                                                                             'stale '
E                                                                             'scratch '
E                                                                             'under the '
E                                                                             'managed '
E                                                                             'SASE temp '
E                                                                             'root\n'
E                                                                             '\n'
E                                                                             'Removes '
E                                                                             'old '
E                                                                             'children '
E                                                                             'from '
E                                                                             'known '
E                                                                             'managed-temp '
E                                                                             'buckets '
E                                                                             'using '
E                                                                             'workload-specific '
E                                                                             'age '
E                                                                             'limits, '
E                                                                             'without '
E                                                                             'following\n'
E                                                                             'symlinks '
E                                                                             'or '
E                                                                             'deleting '
E                                                                             'the '
E                                                                             'stable '
E                                                                             'bucket '
E                                                                             'directories. '
E                                                                             'Each pass '
E                                                                             'removes '
E                                                                             'at most '
E                                                                             '2,000 '
E                                                                             'entries '
E                                                                             'and '
E                                                                             'de-indexes\n'
E                                                                             'deleted '
E                                                                             'agent-artifact '
E                                                                             'directories, '
E                                                                             'so a '

E                                                                             'root '
E                                                                             'converges '
E                                                                             'without '
E                                                                             'blocking '
E                                                                             'interactive '
E                                                                             'commands.',
E                                                              'name': 'managed_tmp_reap',
E                                                              'script': 'sase_chop_managed_tmp_reap'}],
E                                                   'description': 'Run hourly error '
E                                                                  'digests and '
E                                                                  'managed-temp '
E                                                                  'cleanup\n'
E                                                                  '\n'
E                                                                  'Runs once an hour '
E                                                                  'because notification '
E                                                                  'batching and bounded '
E                                                                  'scratch reclamation '
E                                                                  'are useful but not\n'
E                                                                  'latency-sensitive. '
E                                                                  'Put durable '
E                                                                  'maintenance that may '
E                                                                  'scan substantial '
E                                                                  'local state here, '
E                                                                  'not lifecycle,\n'
E                                                                  'dependency, or '
E                                                                  'remote polling work.',
E                                                   'interval': 3600},
E                                  'refresh_docs': {'chops': {'refresh_docs': {'description': 'Refresh '
E                                                                                             'documentation '
E                                                                                             'for '
E                                                                                             'enabled '
E                                                                                             'projects '
E                                                                                             'after '
E                                                                                             'meaningful '
E                                                                                             'repository '
E                                                                                             'drift\n'
E                                                                                             '\n'
E                                                                                             'Runs '
E                                                                                             '`sase_chop_refresh_docs` '
E                                                                                             'every '
E                                                                                             'three '
E                                                                                             'hours '
E                                                                                             'for '
E                                                                                             'each '
E                                                                                             'enabled '
E                                                                                             'project, '
E                                                                                             'but '
E                                                                                             'only '
E                                                                                             'after\n'
E                                                                                             '`git.commits_since` '
E                                                                                             'reports '
E                                                                                             '100 '
E                                                                                             'commits '
E                                                                                             'since '
E                                                                                             'the '
E                                                                                             'last '
E                                                                                             'successful '
E                                                                                             'refresh.\n'
E                                                                                             '\n'
E                                                                                             'The '
E                                                                                             'chop '
E                                                                                             'emits '
E                                                                                             'an '
E                                                                                             'update '
E                                                                                             'proposal '
E                                                                                             'followed '
E                                                                                             'by '
E                                                                                             'a '
E                                                                                             'polish '
E                                                                                             'proposal '
E                                                                                             'that '
E                                                                                             'waits '
E                                                                                             'on '
E                                                                                             'the '
E                                                                                             'update.\n'
E                                                                                             '`checkpoint: '
E                                                                                             'on_action_success` '
E                                                                                             'keeps '
E                                                                                             'the '
E                                                                                             'commit '
E                                                                                             'window '
E                                                                                             'open '
E                                                                                             'until '
E                                                                                             'the '
E                                                                                             'documentation '
E                                                                                             'refresh '
E                                                                                             'actually '
E                                                                                             'lands.',
E                                                                              'for_each': {'source': 'projects'},
E                                                                              'run_every': '3h',
E                                                                              'script': 'sase_chop_refresh_docs',
E                                                                              'trigger': {'git.commits_since': {'checkpoint': 'on_action_success',
E                                                                                                                'project': '{target.name}',
E                                                                                                                'threshold': 100}}}},
E                                                   'description': 'Refresh project '
E                                                                  'documentation after '
E                                                                  'meaningful '
E                                                                  'repository drift\n'
E                                                                  '\n'
E                                                                  'Runs every 60 '
E                                                                  'seconds so each '
E                                                                  'project target can '
E                                                                  'let '
E                                                                  '`git.commits_since` '
E                                                                  'decide when '
E                                                                  'documentation has '
E                                                                  'drifted\n'
E                                                                  'enough to refresh. '
E                                                                  'Documentation-only '
E                                                                  'proposal chops '
E                                                                  'belong here; quality '
E                                                                  'audits and '
E                                                                  'file-splitting '
E                                                                  'maintenance\n'
E                                                                  'stay in their own '
E                                                                  'lanes.\n'
E                                                                  '\n'
E                                                                  'The current chop '
E                                                                  'fans out across '
E                                                                  'enabled projects and '
E                                                                  'checkpoints only '
E                                                                  'after the '
E                                                                  'documentation agents '
E                                                                  'succeed,\n'
E                                                                  'which keeps failed '
E                                                                  'refreshes eligible '
E                                                                  'for a later tick.',
E                                                   'interval': 60,
E                                                   'wait_runners': 0},
E                                  'run_every': {'chops': {'toobig_split': {'description': 'Propose '
E                                                                                          'split-file '
E                                                                                          'agents '
E                                                                                          'for '
E                                                                                          'oversized '
E                                                                                          'Python '
E                                                                                          'files '
E                                                                                          'in '
E                                                                                          'sase\n'
E                                                                                          '\n'
E                                                                                          'Runs '
E                                                                                          '`bugyi_chop_toobig_split` '
E                                                                                          'every '
E                                                                                          '60 '
E                                                                                          'minutes '
E                                                                                          'for '
E                                                                                          'the '
E                                                                                          '`sase` '
E                                                                                          'target, '
E                                                                                          'scanning '
E                                                                                          'the '
E                                                                                          'configured '
E                                                                                          '`src` '
E                                                                                          'and\n'
E                                                                                          '`tests` '
E                                                                                          'trees '

E                                                                                          'line '
E                                                                                          'limits '
E                                                                                          '`1000`, '
E                                                                                          '`850`, '
E                                                                                          'and '

E                                                                                          '\n'
E                                                                                          'The '
E                                                                                          '`toobig-` '
E                                                                                          'clan '
E                                                                                          'inhibit '
E                                                                                          'guard '
E                                                                                          'prevents '
E                                                                                          'overlapping '
E                                                                                          'split-file '
E                                                                                          'swarms. '
E                                                                                          'Each '
E                                                                                          'scan '
E                                                                                          'emits '
E                                                                                          'a '
E                                                                                          'sequential '
E                                                                                          'clan\n'
E                                                                                          'of '
E                                                                                          'content-deduped '
E                                                                                          '`%auto '
E                                                                                          '#split_file:<path>` '
E                                                                                          'proposals, '
E                                                                                          'so '
E                                                                                          'large '
E                                                                                          'files '
E                                                                                          'are '
E                                                                                          'handled '
E                                                                                          'one '
E                                                                                          'at '
E                                                                                          'a '
E                                                                                          'time.',
E                                                                           'for_each': {'names': ['sase'],
E                                                                                        'source': 'projects'},
E                                                                           'inhibit_if': {'agent_clan': {'name_prefix': 'toobig-'}},
E                                                                           'run_every': '60m',
E                                                                           'script': 'bugyi_chop_toobig_split',
E                                                                           'vars': {'limits': ['1000',
E                                                                                               '850',
E                                                                                               '700'],
E                                                                                    'trees': ['src',
E                                                                                              'tests']}}},
E                                                'description': 'Propose scheduled '
E                                                               'maintenance for '
E                                                               'configured projects '
E                                                               'once a minute\n'
E                                                               '\n'
E                                                               'Runs every 60 seconds '
E                                                               'so low-cost maintenance '
E                                                               'checks can notice stale '
E                                                               'conditions without '
E                                                               'waiting for a slower\n'
E                                                               'audit lane. Use this '
E                                                               'lane for chores '
E                                                               'controlled mainly by '
E                                                               '`run_every` and AXE '
E                                                               'inhibit guards, not '
E                                                               'for\n'
E                                                               'commit-threshold '
E                                                               'reviews or '
E                                                               'documentation '
E                                                               'refreshes.\n'
E                                                               '\n'
E                                                               'The current chop '
E                                                               'targets only `sase` and '
E                                                               'is guarded by the '
E                                                               '`toobig-` clan prefix, '
E                                                               'so a previous '
E                                                               'split-file swarm\n'
E                                                               'must clear before '
E                                                               'another scan can '
E                                                               'propose work.',
E                                                'interval': 60,
E                                                'wait_runners': 3},
E                                  'telegram': {'chops': {'tg_inbound': {'description': 'Process '
E                                                                                       'Telegram '
E                                                                                       'inline '
E                                                                                       'keyboard '
E                                                                                       'responses '
E                                                                                       'and '
E                                                                                       'text '
E                                                                                       'messages\n'
E                                                                                       '\n'
E                                                                                       'Polls '
E                                                                                       'the '
E                                                                                       'bot '
E                                                                                       'API '
E                                                                                       'for '
E                                                                                       'callbacks, '
E                                                                                       'slash '
E                                                                                       'commands, '
E                                                                                       'chat '
E                                                                                       'text, '
E                                                                                       'and '
E                                                                                       'attachments, '
E                                                                                       'then '
E                                                                                       'writes '
E                                                                                       'the '
E                                                                                       'matching '
E                                                                                       'SASE\n'
E                                                                                       'response '
E                                                                                       'or '
E                                                                                       'launch '
E                                                                                       'request '
E                                                                                       'for '
E                                                                                       'core '
E                                                                                       'workflows '
E                                                                                       'to '
E                                                                                       'consume.\n'
E                                                                                       '\n'
E                                                                                       'This '
E                                                                                       'chop '
E                                                                                       'runs '
E                                                                                       'on '
E                                                                                       'every '
E                                                                                       'five-second '
E                                                                                       'tick '
E                                                                                       'so '
E                                                                                       'approvals '
E                                                                                       'and '
E                                                                                       'questions '
E                                                                                       'resolve '
E                                                                                       'quickly. '
E                                                                                       'It '
E                                                                                       'uses '
E                                                                                       'the\n'
E                                                                                       'lumberjack-level '
E                                                                                       'bot '
E                                                                                       'environment; '
E                                                                                       'Telegram '
E                                                                                       'launch-disabling '
E                                                                                       'policy '
E                                                                                       'can '
E                                                                                       'still '
E                                                                                       'block '
E                                                                                       'free-form '
E                                                                                       'agent '
E                                                                                       'starts\n'
E                                                                                       'without '
E                                                                                       'stopping '
E                                                                                       'callbacks '
E                                                                                       'or '
E                                                                                       'feedback '
E                                                                                       'responses.',
E                                                                        'script': 'sase_chop_tg_inbound'},
E                                                         'tg_outbound': {'description': 'Send '
E                                                                                        'unread '
E                                                                                        'notifications '
E                                                                                        'to '
E                                                                                        'Telegram\n'
E                                                                                        '\n'
E                                                                                        'Reads '
E                                                                                        'the '
E                                                                                        'same '
E                                                                                        'SASE '
E                                                                                        'notification '
E                                                                                        'store '
E                                                                                        'used '
E                                                                                        'by '
E                                                                                        'ACE, '
E                                                                                        'formats '
E                                                                                        'unread '
E                                                                                        'non-silent '
E                                                                                        'notifications '
E                                                                                        'for '
E                                                                                        'Telegram,\n'

E                                                                                        'generated '
E                                                                                        'images '
E                                                                                        'to '
E                                                                                        'the '
E                                                                                        'configured '
E                                                                                        'chat.\n'
E                                                                                        '\n'
E                                                                                        'The '
E                                                                                        'fast '
E                                                                                        'cadence '
E                                                                                        'keeps '
E                                                                                        'approval '
E                                                                                        'requests '
E                                                                                        'and '
E                                                                                        'completion '
E                                                                                        'notices '
E                                                                                        'visible '
E                                                                                        'without '
E                                                                                        'a '
E                                                                                        'daemon. '
E                                                                                        'Delivery '
E                                                                                        'state '

E                                                                                        'stay '
E                                                                                        'in '
E                                                                                        'the '

E                                                                                        'plugin, '
E                                                                                        'so '

E                                                                                        'AXE '
E                                                                                        'ticks '
E                                                                                        'do '
E                                                                                        'not '
E                                                                                        'resend '
E                                                                                        'handled '
E                                                                                        'notifications.',

E                                               'description': 'Fast lane that relays '
E                                                              'Telegram messages and '
E                                                              'notification delivery '
E                                                              'every few seconds\n'
E                                                              '\n'
E                                                              'Runs every five seconds '
E                                                              'because chat buttons, '
E                                                              'slash commands, and '
E                                                              'outbound notifications '
E                                                              'are useful only when\n'
E                                                              'they feel '
E                                                              'near-real-time. Keep '
E                                                              'Telegram transport chops '
E                                                              'in this lane; broader '
E                                                              'notification maintenance '
E                                                              'belongs\n'
E                                                              'elsewhere so bot network '
E                                                              'calls do not delay '
E                                                              'unrelated work.\n'
E                                                              '\n'
E                                                              'The lumberjack supplies '
E                                                              'the bot username, chat '
E                                                              'id, and token '
E                                                              'environment to both '
E                                                              'chops. It intentionally '
E                                                              'avoids\n'
E                                                              '`run_every` delays so '
E                                                              'inbound decisions and '
E                                                              'outbound '
E                                                              'acknowledgements are '
E                                                              'checked on every tick.',
E                                               'env': {'SASE_TELEGRAM_BOT_CHAT_ID': '8990449281',
E                                                       'SASE_TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN': {'pass': 'telegram_sase_bot_token'},
E                                                       'SASE_TELEGRAM_BOT_USERNAME': 'sase_athena_bot'},
E                                               'interval': 5},
E                                  'waits': {'chops': [{'description': 'Acquire missing '
E                                                                      'bead claims for '
E                                                                      'live pre-launch '
E                                                                      'agents and '
E                                                                      'release claims '
E                                                                      'held by dead '
E                                                                      'ones\n'
E                                                                      '\n'
E                                                                      'Scans pre-launch '
E                                                                      'agent artifacts, '
E                                                                      'backfills a '
E                                                                      'missing claim '
E                                                                      'for a live '
E                                                                      'waiting agent, '
E                                                                      'and releases a\n'
E                                                                      'claimed bead '
E                                                                      'when its '
E                                                                      'unpromoted owner '
E                                                                      'has died. '
E                                                                      'Reconciled dead '
E                                                                      'records are '
E                                                                      'tombstoned so '
E                                                                      'later ticks '
E                                                                      'avoid\n'
E                                                                      'reopening their '
E                                                                      'stores, while a '
E                                                                      'failed project '
E                                                                      'read is retried '
E                                                                      'safely.',
E                                                       'name': 'bead_claim_checks',
E                                                       'script': 'sase_chop_bead_claim_checks'},
E                                                      {'description': 'Integrate the '
E                                                                      'canonical bead '
E                                                                      'store for '
E                                                                      'projects with '
E                                                                      'agents waiting '
E                                                                      'on beads\n'
E                                                                      '\n'
E                                                                      'Finds projects '
E                                                                      'with a live '
E                                                                      'agent waiting on '
E                                                                      'bead completion '
E                                                                      'and refreshes '
E                                                                      'each canonical '
E                                                                      'store at most\n'
E                                                                      'every 30 '
E                                                                      'seconds. The '
E                                                                      'two-minute '
E                                                                      'timeout is '
E                                                                      'protected by '
E                                                                      'bounded '
E                                                                      'per-project lock '
E                                                                      'waits, a '
E                                                                      'whole-pass work\n'
E                                                                      'budget, and '
E                                                                      'persistent '
E                                                                      'exponential '
E                                                                      'backoff so one '
E                                                                      'contended store '
E                                                                      'cannot stall all '
E                                                                      'waiters.',
E                                                       'name': 'bead_store_refresh',
E                                                       'run_every': '30s',
E                                                       'script': 'sase_chop_bead_store_refresh',
E                                                       'timeout': '2m'},
E                                                      {'description': 'Flush planner '
E                                                                      'completion '
E                                                                      'notifications '
E                                                                      'orphaned by an '
E                                                                      'unsettled epic '
E                                                                      'launch\n'
E                                                                      '\n'
E                                                                      'Preserves '
E                                                                      'deferrals while '
E                                                                      'a matching '
E                                                                      'detached '
E                                                                      'epic-launch task '
E                                                                      'is active, '
E                                                                      'flushes unowned '
E                                                                      'deferrals after\n'
E                                                                      'a 90-second '
E                                                                      'grace period '
E                                                                      'with a resume '
E                                                                      'command, and '
E                                                                      'reaps unclaimed '
E                                                                      'settle markers '
E                                                                      'after one hour.',
E                                                       'name': 'epic_launch_flush',
E                                                       'run_every': '30s',
E                                                       'script': 'sase_chop_epic_launch_flush'},
E                                                      {'description': 'Fetch and '
E                                                                      'fast-forward '
E                                                                      'opted-in primary '
E                                                                      'sidecar clones '
E                                                                      '(plans, beads, '
E                                                                      'research, '
E                                                                      'custom)\n'
E                                                                      '\n'
E                                                                      'Scans every '
E                                                                      'enabled '
E                                                                      "project's "
E                                                                      'auto_sync '
E                                                                      'sidecar roles, '
E                                                                      'syncing a role '
E                                                                      'immediately when '
E                                                                      'a publisher left '
E                                                                      'a\n'
E                                                                      'pending hint and '
E                                                                      'otherwise '
E                                                                      'backstopping it '
E                                                                      'at most every '
E                                                                      'five minutes. '
E                                                                      'Only a clean, '
E                                                                      'attached, '
E                                                                      'non-diverged\n'
E                                                                      'clone with a '
E                                                                      'matching remote '
E                                                                      'is fetched and '
E                                                                      'fast-forwarded; '
E                                                                      'dirty, detached, '
E                                                                      'diverged, '
E                                                                      'mismatched, '
E                                                                      'missing,\n'
E                                                                      'or busy clones '
E                                                                      'are left '
E                                                                      'untouched and '
E                                                                      'reported. '
E                                                                      'Bounded work '
E                                                                      'budget and '
E                                                                      'persistent '
E                                                                      'per-role backoff '
E                                                                      'keep one\n'
E                                                                      'unhealthy clone '
E                                                                      'from stalling '
E                                                                      'the rest.',
E                                                       'name': 'sidecar_auto_sync',
E                                                       'run_every': '30s',
E                                                       'script': 'sase_chop_sidecar_auto_sync',
E                                                       'timeout': '2m'},
E                                                      {'description': 'Resolve agent '
E                                                                      'wait '
E                                                                      'dependencies and '
E                                                                      'write ready.json '
E                                                                      'when satisfied\n'
E                                                                      '\n'
E                                                                      'Scans waiting '
E                                                                      'markers across '
E                                                                      'projects and '
E                                                                      'resolves '
E                                                                      'named-agent, '
E                                                                      'artifact, and '
E                                                                      'closed-bead '
E                                                                      'dependencies '
E                                                                      'from\n'
E                                                                      'shared agent '
E                                                                      'metadata and '
E                                                                      'canonical bead '
E                                                                      'state. It writes '
E                                                                      'ready.json only '
E                                                                      'after every '
E                                                                      'dependency is\n'
E                                                                      'satisfied; '
E                                                                      'invalid or '
E                                                                      'already-ready '
E                                                                      'markers are '
E                                                                      'skipped without '
E                                                                      'blocking other '
E                                                                      'agents.',
E                                                       'name': 'wait_checks',
E                                                       'script': 'sase_chop_wait_checks'}],
E                                            'description': 'Resolve agent wait '
E                                                           'dependencies and keep bead '
E                                                           'claims and stores in sync\n'
E                                                           '\n'
E                                                           'Runs every ten seconds so '
E                                                           'waiting agents resume '
E                                                           'promptly and short-lived '
E                                                           'bead claims are reconciled '
E                                                           'quickly.\n'
E                                                           'Put agent dependency, '
E                                                           'bead-claim, and bead-store '
E                                                           'coordination here; Patch '
E                                                           'lifecycle checks and '
E                                                           'general\n'
E                                                           'cleanup belong in their '
E                                                           'dedicated lanes.',
E                                            'interval': 10}},

E                           'retry': {'claude': {'error_patterns': ['API Error: 500',
E                                                                   'API Error: 529',
E                                                                   'Internal server '

E                                     'codex': {'error_patterns': ['exceeded retry limit',
