E                                                                  'Selected model is at '
E                                                                  'capacity'],
E                                               'max_retries': 3,
E                                               'wait_times': [60, 300, 1800]},
E                                     'sase': {'error_patterns': ['uses a format this '
E                                                                 'process does not '
E                                                                 'understand'],
E                                              'max_retries': 1,
E                                              'preserve_workspace': True,
E                                              'spawn_new_agent': True,
E                                              'wait_times': [0]}}},
E          'markdown': {'print_width': 88},
E          'max_running_agents': 10,
E          'memory': {'agents_minimal_template': None,
E                     'agents_template': None,
E                     'glossary': {'Agent Clan': {'definition': 'An agent clan is a '
E                                                               'named, rootless '
E                                                               'container for agents '
E                                                               'that run in parallel. '
E                                                               'Every member is named '
E                                                               "inside the clan's hood "
E                                                               '(`<clan>.<suffix>`) and '
E                                                               'declares '
E                                                               '`%clan:<clan>`; the '
E                                                               'clan name is reserved '
E                                                               'and is never itself an '
E                                                               'agent.'},
E                                  'Agent Family': {'definition': 'An agent family is a '
E                                                                 'sase agent whose '
E                                                                 'agent shells run as a '
E                                                                 'strictly sequential '
E                                                                 'chain. Its shells use '
E                                                                 '`<family>--<suffix>` '
E                                                                 'names. The first '
E                                                                 '`%id(parent, suffix)` '
E                                                                 'attachment renames '
E                                                                 'the original shell '
E                                                                 'with its own suffix '
E                                                                 'and reserves the bare '
E                                                                 'family name as the '
E                                                                 'sase agent container, '
E                                                                 'so a family always '
E                                                                 'has at least two '
E                                                                 'shells.'},
E                                  'Agent Hood': {'aliases': ['hood',
E                                                             'agent neighborhood'],
E                                                 'definition': 'An agent hood is a '
E                                                               'group of agents that '
E                                                               'are all named with the '
E                                                               'same `<name>.` prefix. '
E                                                               'For example, agents '
E                                                               'named `foo.bar`, '
E                                                               '`foo.baz`, and '
E                                                               '`foo.bar.1` are all '
E                                                               'apart of the same `foo` '
E                                                               'agent hood. The agent '
E                                                               '`foo`, if it exists, is '
E                                                               'also considered part of '
E                                                               'the `foo` agent hood.'},
E                                  'Agent Instruction File': {'aliases': ['agents.md '
E                                                                         'file'],
E                                                             'definition': 'An agent '
E                                                                           'instruction '
E                                                                           'file is a '
E                                                                           '`.md` file '
E                                                                           'that an '
E                                                                           'agent CLI '
E                                                                           'reads '
E                                                                           'automatically '
E                                                                           'when '
E                                                                           'working in '
E                                                                           'a directory '
E                                                                           'that '
E                                                                           'contains '
E                                                                           'it. For '
E                                                                           'example, '
E                                                                           'the '
E                                                                           '`AGENTS.md` '
E                                                                           'file is the '
E                                                                           'name of the '
E                                                                           'agent '
E                                                                           'instruction '
E                                                                           'file that '
E                                                                           'is '
E                                                                           'supported '
E                                                                           'by codex. '
E                                                                           'sase '
E                                                                           'supports '
E                                                                           'one agent '
E                                                                           'instruction '
E                                                                           'file per '
E                                                                           'supported '
E                                                                           'agent CLI '
E                                                                           '(ex: '
E                                                                           '`CLAUDE.md` '
E                                                                           'for claude, '
E                                                                           '`GEMINI.md` '
E                                                                           'for '
E                                                                           'antigravity, '
E                                                                           'etc...). '
E                                                                           'The `sase '
E                                                                           'init` '
E                                                                           'command, '
E                                                                           'which is '
E                                                                           'run '
E                                                                           'automatically '
E                                                                           'as a sase '
E                                                                           'post-commit '
E                                                                           'hook, '
E                                                                           'initializes '
E                                                                           'the '
E                                                                           'top-level '
E                                                                           'agent '
E                                                                           'instruction '
E                                                                           'files using '
E                                                                           'memories in '
E                                                                           'the '
E                                                                           'sase/memory/ '
E                                                                           'directory '
E                                                                           'and ensures '
E                                                                           'that all '
E                                                                           'agent '
E                                                                           'instruction '
E                                                                           'files in '
E                                                                           'the same '
E                                                                           'directory '
E                                                                           'contain the '
E                                                                           'same '
E                                                                           'contents.'},
E                                  'Agent Lane': {'definition': 'An agent lane is the '
E                                                               'display container for a '
E                                                               'non-dismissed sase '
E                                                               'agent: its agent '
E                                                               'family, or its agent '
E                                                               'shell when it has no '
E                                                               'family. Dismissal '
E                                                               'removes the lane, not '
E                                                               "the sase agent's "
E                                                               'identity.'},
E                                  'Agent Neighbor': {'definition': 'An agent neighbor '
E                                                                   'is any agent that '
E                                                                   'is in the same '
E                                                                   'agent hood as '
E                                                                   'another agent. For '
E                                                                   'example, agents '
E                                                                   'named `foo`, '
E                                                                   '`foo.baz`, and '
E                                                                   '`foo.bar.1` are all '
E                                                                   'neighbors of each '
E                                                                   'other because they '
E                                                                   'are all in the same '
E                                                                   '`foo` agent hood.'},
E                                  'Agent Shell': {'definition': 'An agent shell is one '
E                                                                'concrete LLM/provider '
E                                                                'run. It holds a '
E                                                                'workspace claim while '
E                                                                'active and is the '
E                                                                'execution row shown by '
E                                                                '`sase agent list`.'},
E                                  'Agent Tribe': {'definition': 'An agent tribe is a '
E                                                                'user-facing label for '
E                                                                'related agents across '
E                                                                'clans and families. '
E                                                                'Assign a tribe at '
E                                                                'launch with '
E                                                                '`%id(tribe=<tribe>)` '
E                                                                'or `#tribe:<tribe>` '
E                                                                'for an auto-named '
E                                                                'agent, `%id(<id>, '
E                                                                'tribe=<tribe>)` for an '
E                                                                'explicitly named '
E                                                                'agent, or '
E                                                                '`%clan(<clan>, '
E                                                                'tribe=<tribe>)` for a '
E                                                                'clan. Tribes can also '
E                                                                'be managed after '
E                                                                'launch with `sase '
E                                                                'agent tribe` and are '
E                                                                'displayed with an `@` '
E                                                                'prefix.'},
E                                  'Artifact Reference': {'aliases': ['ref'],
E                                                         'definition': 'An artifact '
E                                                                       'reference (ref) '
E                                                                       'is a typed '
E                                                                       '`@<kind>:<argument>` '
E                                                                       'citation in an '
E                                                                       'agent prompt. '
E                                                                       'Builtin kinds '
E                                                                       'are `@stitch`, '
E                                                                       '`@patch`, '
E                                                                       '`@bead`, '
E                                                                       '`@agent`, and '
E                                                                       'the special '
E                                                                       '`@file`; '
E                                                                       'artifact repos '
E                                                                       'add document '
E                                                                       'kinds such as '
E                                                                       '`@plan` and '
E                                                                       '`@research` '
E                                                                       'through a '
E                                                                       "project's "
E                                                                       '`ref:` config, '
E                                                                       'written inline '
E                                                                       'or with `use: '
E                                                                       '<provider>` '
E                                                                       'from an '
E                                                                       'installed '
E                                                                       'provider '
E                                                                       'plugin. Every '
E                                                                       'ref expands to '
E                                                                       'prompt text, is '
E                                                                       'recorded '
E                                                                       'against the '
E                                                                       'agent that used '
E                                                                       'it, and '
E                                                                       'publishes as a '
E                                                                       '`[@kind:arg][N]` '
E                                                                       'link.'},
E                                  'Patch': {'definition': "A Patch is SASE's local unit "
E                                                          'of change. Every PR created '
E                                                          'or managed by SASE is '
E                                                          'associated with exactly one '
E                                                          'Patch, but a Patch may exist '
E                                                          'without a PR, represented by '
E                                                          'an absent `PR:` field. '
E                                                          'Active Patches live in '
E                                                          'ProjectSpec `<key>.sase` '
E                                                          '(directory key `<key>`; see '
E                                                          'Project, Repo, and '
E                                                          'Workspace); terminal ones '
E                                                          '(Submitted, Archived, '
E                                                          'Reverted) live in '
E                                                          '`<key>-archive.sase`. '
E                                                          'Sections: NAME, DESCRIPTION, '
E                                                          'PARENT, PR, STATUS, '
E                                                          'STITCHES, HOOKS, COMMENTS, '
E                                                          'MENTORS. Status lifecycle: '
E                                                          'WIP -> Draft -> Ready -> '
E                                                          'Mailed -> Submitted.'},
E                                  'Proc': {'aliases': ['procs',
E                                                       'background task',
E                                                       'background tasks'],
E                                           'definition': 'A Proc is a durable '
E                                                         'background process SASE '
E                                                         'records, supervises, and can '
E                                                         'stream or kill. Procs live in '
E                                                         '`~/.sase/procs/procs.jsonl` '
E                                                         'with combined output logs and '
E                                                         'are surfaced by `sase proc` '
E                                                         "and ACE's Procs tab. "
E                                                         'Historical proc rows may use '
E                                                         '`command`, `tui`, or '
E                                                         '`detached` kind values, but '
E                                                         'those names are compatibility '
E                                                         'labels rather than permanent '
E                                                         'semantic categories. Distinct '
E                                                         'from a task bead, which is a '
E                                                         'work item, and from an '
E                                                         'asyncio task.'},
E                                  'Proc Shell': {'definition': 'A proc shell is a named '
E                                                               'supervised proc '
E                                                               'belonging to a sase '
E                                                               'agent, with durable '
E                                                               'output and lifecycle '
E                                                               'state. A '
E                                                               'family-attached proc '
E                                                               'shell is a monitor and '
E                                                               'may carry timeout, '
E                                                               'workspace-claim, and '
E                                                               'follow-up policy.'},
E                                  'Sase Agent': {'aliases': ['agent'],
E                                                 'definition': 'A sase agent is an '
E                                                               'agent family or a '
E                                                               'single agent that does '
E                                                               'not belong to a family. '
E                                                               'It owns an ordered '
E                                                               'sequence of sase '
E                                                               'shells, and its name '
E                                                               'never ends in '
E                                                               '`--<suffix>` because '
E                                                               'that suffix is reserved '
E                                                               'for agent shells. A '
E                                                               'one-shell agent may '
E                                                               'share its shell name, '
E                                                               'while a family uses the '
E                                                               'bare name for its '
E                                                               'container.'},
E                                  'Sase Project': {'aliases': ['project'],
E                                                   'definition': 'A sase project is a '
E                                                                 'named unit of work '
E                                                                 'registered with SASE. '
E                                                                 'A project is created '
E                                                                 'only when a new VCS '
E                                                                 'xprompt argument '
E                                                                 'resolves to a valid '
E                                                                 'project: '
E                                                                 '`#git:<name>` accepts '
E                                                                 'any valid project '
E                                                                 'name, while '
E                                                                 '`#gh:<org>/<repo>` '
E                                                                 'requires an existing '
E                                                                 'GitHub repository. '
E                                                                 'Its ProjectSpec is '
E                                                                 '`~/.sase/projects/<key>/<key>.sase`, '
E                                                                 'where the directory '
E                                                                 'key `<key>` is '
E                                                                 '`<name>` for `#git` '
E                                                                 'projects but '
E                                                                 '`gh_<org>__<repo>` '
E                                                                 'for `#gh` projects '
E                                                                 '(ex: '
E                                                                 '`gh_sase-org__sase`); '
E                                                                 'the user-facing name '
E                                                                 "is the spec's "
E                                                                 '`PROJECT_NAME:` (ex: '
E                                                                 '`sase`) or, if unset, '
E                                                                 'the key. Projects '
E                                                                 'have exactly two '
E                                                                 'user-facing states, '
E                                                                 'enabled and disabled; '
E                                                                 'missing '
E                                                                 '`PROJECT_STATE:` '
E                                                                 'means enabled, and '
E                                                                 'only an explicit '
E                                                                 'disable changes that. '
E                                                                 'The system-managed '
E                                                                 '`home` project '
E                                                                 'remains hidden.'},
E                                  'Sase Repo': {'aliases': ['repo'],
E                                                'definition': 'A sase repo is any '
E                                                              'repository SASE knows: a '
E                                                              "project's primary repo, "
E                                                              'an artifact sidecar repo '
E                                                              'such as '
E                                                              '`<project>--plans` or '
E                                                              '`<project>--research`, '
E                                                              'or a repo declared '
E                                                              'through `repos.linked`.'},
E                                  'Sase Shell': {'aliases': ['shell'],
E                                                 'definition': 'A sase shell is one '
E                                                               'executing member of a '
E                                                               'sase agent, either an '
E                                                               'agent shell or a proc '
E                                                               'shell. A sase agent is '
E                                                               'a sequence of sase '
E                                                               'shells.'},
E                                  'Sase Workspace': {'aliases': ['workspace'],
E                                                     'definition': 'A sase workspace is '
E                                                                   'a numbered clone of '
E                                                                   "a project's primary "
E                                                                   'repo, managed by '
E                                                                   'the workspace store '
E                                                                   'and tracked in that '
E                                                                   "project's "
E                                                                   '`registry.json`. '
E                                                                   'Each SASE agent '
E                                                                   'claims exactly one '
E                                                                   'workspace until '
E                                                                   'completion. '
E                                                                   'Workspace '
E                                                                   'directories are not '
E                                                                   'repos. Linked-repo '
E                                                                   'clones materialized '
E                                                                   'for a workspace are '
E                                                                   'repo checkouts, not '
E                                                                   'additional '
E                                                                   'workspaces.'},
E                                  'Stitch': {'definition': 'A stitch is the lightweight '
E                                                           'ordered change record '
E                                                           "inside a Patch's "
E                                                           '`STITCHES:` section. Every '
E                                                           'VCS commit made through the '
E                                                           'tracked workflow has an '
E                                                           'associated numeric stitch, '
E                                                           'but a stitch need not have '
E                                                           'a commit: proposals retain '
E                                                           'numeric-plus-letter IDs '
E                                                           'such as `(2a)`. The `sase '
E                                                           'commit` command and real '
E                                                           'Git/Mercurial commits are '
E                                                           'still called commits.'},
E                                  'Xprompt': {'definition': 'Triggered with `#foo` in '
E                                                            'agent prompts. Defined in '
E                                                            'a sase/xprompts/ directory '
E                                                            '(.md or .yml file) or in '
E                                                            '~/.config/sase/sase.yml '
E                                                            '(`xprompts` field).'},
E                                  'Xprompt Memory': {'aliases': ['memory file'],
E                                                     'definition': 'A flat SASE memory '
E                                                                   'note exposed as a '
E                                                                   'namespaced xprompt: '
E                                                                   '`sase/memory/foo.md` '
E                                                                   'expands with '
E                                                                   '`#memory/foo`, and '
E                                                                   'the `memory/` '
E                                                                   'prefix is '
E                                                                   'required.'},
E                                  'Xprompt Part': {'definition': '.md file -> single '
E                                                                 '`prompt_part` step '
E                                                                 "with the file's "
E                                                                 'content.'},
E                                  'Xprompt Swarm': {'definition': 'An xprompt whose '
E                                                                  'body contains '
E                                                                  'top-level `---` '
E                                                                  'segment separators '
E                                                                  'outside fenced '
E                                                                  'blocks and fans out '
E                                                                  'into one agent per '
E                                                                  'segment at launch. '
E                                                                  'Literal user prompts '
E                                                                  'can also use `---`, '
E                                                                  'but those are '
E                                                                  'generic multi-agent '
E                                                                  'prompts rather than '
E                                                                  'xprompt swarms.'},
E                                  'Xprompt Workflow': {'definition': '.yml file -> '
E                                                                     'multiple steps '
E                                                                     '(`prompt_part`, '
E                                                                     '`python`, `bash`, '
E                                                                     'etc.).'}},
E                     'h1_title': 'Structured Agentic Software Engineering (SASE) - '

E                     'readme_template': None,

E                       'bd/land_epic': {'content': 'You are the land agent for epic '
E                                                   'bead {{ bead_id }}: verify the epic '
E                                                   'is truly complete, integrate it '
E                                                   'with changes\n'
E                                                   'that landed since it started, then '
E                                                   'close it out.\n'
E                                                   '\n'
E                                                   '1. Verify. Run `sase bead show {{ '
E                                                   'bead_id }}` (children, linked plan '
E                                                   "file), review the epic bead's own "
E                                                   'notes, then\n'
E                                                   '   run `sase bead show` on every '
E                                                   'child and review every child note. '
E                                                   'Confirm each note was addressed, '
E                                                   'and read the\n'
E                                                   '   actual source code and the '
E                                                   "epic's commits (bead IDs appear in "
E                                                   'commit messages) to confirm the '
E                                                   'work previous\n'
E                                                   '   agents reported complete really '
E                                                   'is. While reviewing child beads, '
E                                                   'collect every `PROPOSED FOLLOW-UP:` '
E                                                   'note entry.\n'
E                                                   '\n'
E                                                   '2. Integrate. Changes committed '
E                                                   'since this epic started could not '
E                                                   "integrate with this epic's feature "
E                                                   'while it was\n'
E                                                   '   incomplete. Find them (e.g. `git '
E                                                   'log` since the first commit '
E                                                   'mentioning {{ bead_id }}, excluding '
E                                                   "the epic's own\n"
E                                                   '   commits; in a PR workflow also '
E                                                   'review commits on the base branch) '
E                                                   'and update anything that should now '
E                                                   'use what\n'
E                                                   '   this epic added or that '
E                                                   'duplicates or conflicts with it. '
E                                                   'This integration is part of the '
E                                                   "epic's work.\n"
E                                                   '\n'
E                                                   '3. Land. Unresolved issues caused '
E                                                   'by this epic remain epic work: plan '
E                                                   'and finish them before closing. For '
E                                                   'each\n'
E                                                   '   genuinely distinct follow-up '
E                                                   'that is not caused by the epic, use '
E                                                   '`/sase_new_task` with details '
E                                                   'identifying the\n'
E                                                   '   proposing bead; it will '
E                                                   'corroborate a duplicate, attach a '
E                                                   'causally related active-epic issue, '
E                                                   'or create a sized\n'
E                                                   '   task as appropriate. Record '
E                                                   'every outcome, including why any '
E                                                   'proposal was declined, in your '
E                                                   'close note. Close\n'
E                                                   '   the epic with `sase bead close '
E                                                   '{{ bead_id }} --note "<what you '
E                                                   'verified in steps 1-2>"`. AFTER '
E                                                   'closing, run\n'
E                                                   '   `just symvision` if available '
E                                                   '(epic-symbol whitelist entries for '
E                                                   '{{ bead_id }} expire at close) and '
E                                                   'remove the\n'
E                                                   '   stale entries and unused code it '
E                                                   'reports. Finally, set `status: '
E                                                   'done` in the frontmatter of the '
E                                                   "epic's plan\n"
E                                                   '   file (the PLAN path shown by '
E                                                   '`sase bead show`). If the close is '
E                                                   'rejected, the named phases were '
E                                                   'never\n'
E                                                   '   completed: finish or reopen '
E                                                   'them, or record the outcome '
E                                                   'deliberately with\n'
E                                                   '   `--force --reason ... '
E                                                   '--resolution canceled|superseded`. '
E                                                   'Never force merely to make the '
E                                                   'command succeed, and\n'
E                                                   '   never use `--force` to advance a '
E                                                   'successful nested landing.\n'
E                                                   '\n'
E                                                   'If steps 1-2 uncover remaining '
E                                                   'work, use your /sase_plan skill to '
E                                                   "plan it and complete the skill's "
E                                                   'tier-aware\n'
E                                                   'validate/revalidate/propose loop. '
E                                                   'Plan only the remaining work. Do '
E                                                   "not include this epic's close, "
E                                                   'symvision pass,\n'
E                                                   'or plan-file status update as a '
E                                                   "child phase; the child epic's "
E                                                   '`parent_bead` link is the handoff '
E                                                   'that lets its land\n'
E                                                   'agent resume this interrupted '
E                                                   'landing after the child lands.\n'
E                                                   '\n'
E                                                   'After the current epic closes, '
E                                                   'inspect the linked `parent_bead` '
E                                                   'from `sase bead show {{ bead_id '
E                                                   '}}`. If there is\n'
E                                                   'no parent bead, finish normally. If '
E                                                   'the parent is a phase bead, verify '
E                                                   'this child plan completed the work '
E                                                   'required\n'
E                                                   'by that phase, close only that '
E                                                   'parent phase normally with `sase '
E                                                   'bead close <parent-bead> --note '
E                                                   '"<what you\n'
E                                                   'verified>"`, and leave the '
E                                                   'containing epic to its '
E                                                   'already-waiting land agent. If the '
E                                                   'parent is a plan bead, review\n'
E                                                   "the parent's previous landing note, "
E                                                   'all descendants and notes, linked '
E                                                   'plan file, and post-child drift; '
E                                                   'rerun\n'
E                                                   'descendant and linked-plan '
E                                                   'readiness checks before closing it. '
E                                                   'When the parent plan is still '
E                                                   'complete, close it\n'
E                                                   'normally with `sase bead close '
E                                                   '<parent-bead> --note "<what you '
E                                                   'rechecked>"`, run its post-close '
E                                                   'symvision cleanup,\n'
E                                                   'mark its linked plan file done, and '
E                                                   'then repeat through directly '
E                                                   'parented plan ancestors while each '
E                                                   'remains fully\n'
E                                                   'complete. Stop at the first '
E                                                   'incomplete or ambiguous parent, '
E                                                   'record a note on that parent '
E                                                   'describing the blocker,\n'
E                                                   'and report it in your final '
E                                                   'response.\n',
E                                        'input': {'bead_id': 'word'},
E                                        'tags': 'land_epic'},
E                       'bd/review/plan': {'content': 'Can you review the requirements '
E                                                     'in the canonical prompt archive '
E                                                     'entry for {{ file_base }} against '
E                                                     'the plan in the\n'
E                                                     '@sdd/plans/*/{{ file_base }}.md '
E                                                     'file and ensure that the '
E                                                     'following qualities are met? Use\n'
E                                                     '`sase agent prompts show {{ '
E                                                     'file_base }}` if the prompt is '
E                                                     'not already in context.\n'
E                                                     '\n'
E                                                     '  1. ALL requirements in the '
E                                                     'prompt file have been satisfied.\n'
E                                                     '  2. Each phase of the plan is '
E                                                     'sufficiently detailed.\n'
E                                                     '  3. ALL necessary design '
E                                                     'decisions have been made and '
E                                                     'documented in the plan.\n'
E                                                     '\n'
E                                                     'If there are any deficiencies in '
E                                                     'the current plan, you should '
E                                                     'update the plan file to properly '
E                                                     'address them.\n',
E                                          'input': {'file_base': 'word'}},
E                       'bd/review/prompt': {'content': 'Can you help me review the '
E                                                       "prompt I've prepared in the "
E                                                       'canonical prompt archive entry '
E                                                       'for {{ file_base }} and look '
E                                                       'for\n'
E                                                       'gaps / ambiguity in the '

E                                                       'prompts show {{ file_base }}` '
E                                                       'if the prompt is not already '

E                                            'input': {'file_base': 'word'}},
E                       'bd/review_tasks': {'content': 'Can you review all of the '
E                                                      'current task sase beads that are '
E                                                      'opened for\n'
E                                                      'the "{{ project }}" project? '
E                                                      'There are currently too many '
E                                                      'open task beads for me to\n'
E                                                      'review or approve right now. Can '
E                                                      'you help me complete the '
E                                                      'following sequence of\n'
E                                                      'steps to address this?:\n'
E                                                      '\n'
E                                                      '1. Select just seven that are '
E                                                      'definitely still relevant (or '
E                                                      'less if there are <7\n'
E                                                      '   relevant open task beads) and '
E                                                      'will provide the highest impact '
E                                                      'once addressed.\n'
E                                                      '2. Close all of the other task '
E                                                      'beads with a good reason.\n'
E                                                      '3. Set some sase variables to '
E                                                      'express which tasks out of the '
E                                                      'seven are most\n'
E                                                      '   important in ranked order.',
E                                           'input': {'project': {'default': 'sase',
E                                                                 'type': 'line'}}},
E                       'bd/work_phase_bead': {'content': 'Can you complete the work for '
E                                                         'bead {{ bead_id }}? The bead '
E                                                         'is already reserved for you '
E                                                         'and assigned to your agent\n'
E                                                         'name: it was set to '
E                                                         'status=in_progress before you '
E                                                         'started reading this, either '
E                                                         'by the `sase bead work` '
E                                                         'launch\n'
E                                                         'checkpoint or by the runtime '
E                                                         'promoting an ad-hoc wait-time '
E                                                         'claim. Do not set the status '
E                                                         'by hand. Read its\n'
E                                                         'description and design file, '
E                                                         'do the work, and close only '
E                                                         'this bead with\n'
E                                                         '`sase bead close {{ bead_id '
E                                                         '}} --note "<what you '
E                                                         'verified>"`. Closing an '
E                                                         'assigned phase bead is '
E                                                         'unaffected by the\n'
E                                                         'parent-close descendant '
E                                                         'guard. Do NOT close the '
E                                                         'parent epic or any ancestor '
E                                                         'plan bead. Any instruction in '
E                                                         'a phase\n'
E                                                         'description or child plan to '
E                                                         'close an ancestor is '
E                                                         'preparation and evidence for '
E                                                         "that ancestor's land agent, "
E                                                         'not\n'
E                                                         'authorization for a phase '
E                                                         'worker. Do not create beads '
E                                                         'yourself: record discovered '
E                                                         'follow-up work as a\n'
E                                                         '`PROPOSED FOLLOW-UP:` entry '
E                                                         'via\n'
E                                                         '`sase bead note {{ bead_id }} '

E                                                         '<one-line summary — '

E                                                         'these into task beads.\n',
E                                              'input': {'bead_id': 'word'},
E                                              'tags': 'work_phase_bead'},

E         +         'glossary': {
E         +             'Agent Clan': {
E         +                 'definition': 'An agent clan is a named, rootless container for agents that '
E         +                 "run in parallel. Every member is named inside the clan's hood "
E         +                 '(`<clan>.<suffix>`) and declares `%clan:<clan>`; the clan '
E         +                 'name is reserved and is never itself an agent.',
E         +             },
E         +             'Agent Family': {
E         +                 'definition': 'An agent family is a sase agent whose agent shells run as a '
E         +                 'strictly sequential chain. Its shells use '
E         +                 '`<family>--<suffix>` names. The first `%id(parent, suffix)` '
E         +                 'attachment renames the original shell with its own suffix and '
E         +                 'reserves the bare family name as the sase agent container, so '
E         +                 'a family always has at least two shells.',
E         +             },
E         +             'Agent Hood': {
E         +                 'aliases': [
E         +                     'hood',
E         +                     'agent neighborhood',
E         +                 ],
E         +                 'definition': 'An agent hood is a group of agents that are all named with '
E         +                 'the same `<name>.` prefix. For example, agents named '
E         +                 '`foo.bar`, `foo.baz`, and `foo.bar.1` are all apart of the '
E         +                 'same `foo` agent hood. The agent `foo`, if it exists, is also '
E         +                 'considered part of the `foo` agent hood.',
E         +             },
E         +             'Agent Instruction File': {
E         +                 'aliases': [
E         +                     'agents.md file',
E         +                 ],
E         +                 'definition': 'An agent instruction file is a `.md` file that an agent CLI '
E         +                 'reads automatically when working in a directory that contains '
E         +                 'it. For example, the `AGENTS.md` file is the name of the '
E         +                 'agent instruction file that is supported by codex. sase '
E         +                 'supports one agent instruction file per supported agent CLI '
E         +                 '(ex: `CLAUDE.md` for claude, `GEMINI.md` for antigravity, '
E         +                 'etc...). The `sase init` command, which is run automatically '
E         +                 'as a sase post-commit hook, initializes the top-level agent '
E         +                 'instruction files using memories in the sase/memory/ '
E         +                 'directory and ensures that all agent instruction files in the '
E         +                 'same directory contain the same contents.',
E         +             },
E         +             'Agent Lane': {
E         +                 'definition': 'An agent lane is the display container for a non-dismissed '
E         +                 'sase agent: its agent family, or its agent shell when it has '
E         +                 "no family. Dismissal removes the lane, not the sase agent's "
E         +                 'identity.',
E         +             },
E         +             'Agent Neighbor': {
E         +                 'definition': 'An agent neighbor is any agent that is in the same agent hood '
E         +                 'as another agent. For example, agents named `foo`, `foo.baz`, '
E         +                 'and `foo.bar.1` are all neighbors of each other because they '
E         +                 'are all in the same `foo` agent hood.',
E         +             },
E         +             'Agent Shell': {
E         +                 'definition': 'An agent shell is one concrete LLM/provider run. It holds a '
E         +                 'workspace claim while active and is the execution row shown '
E         +                 'by `sase agent list`.',
E         +             },
E         +             'Agent Tribe': {
E         +                 'definition': 'An agent tribe is a user-facing label for related agents '
E         +                 'across clans and families. Assign a tribe at launch with '
E         +                 '`%id(tribe=<tribe>)` or `#tribe:<tribe>` for an auto-named '
E         +                 'agent, `%id(<id>, tribe=<tribe>)` for an explicitly named '
E         +                 'agent, or `%clan(<clan>, tribe=<tribe>)` for a clan. Tribes '
E         +                 'can also be managed after launch with `sase agent tribe` and '
E         +                 'are displayed with an `@` prefix.',
E         +             },
E         +             'Artifact Reference': {
E         +                 'aliases': [
E         +                     'ref',
E         +                 ],
E         +                 'definition': 'An artifact reference (ref) is a typed `@<kind>:<argument>` '
E         +                 'citation in an agent prompt. Builtin kinds are `@stitch`, '
E         +                 '`@patch`, `@bead`, `@agent`, and the special `@file`; '
E         +                 'artifact repos add document kinds such as `@plan` and '
E         +                 "`@research` through a project's `ref:` config, written inline "
E         +                 'or with `use: <provider>` from an installed provider plugin. '
E         +                 'Every ref expands to prompt text, is recorded against the '
E         +                 'agent that used it, and publishes as a `[@kind:arg][N]` link.',
E         +             },
E         +             'Patch': {
E         +                 'definition': "A Patch is SASE's local unit of change. Every PR created or "
E         +                 'managed by SASE is associated with exactly one Patch, but a '
E         +                 'Patch may exist without a PR, represented by an absent `PR:` '
E         +                 'field. Active Patches live in ProjectSpec `<key>.sase` '
E         +                 '(directory key `<key>`; see Project, Repo, and Workspace); '
E         +                 'terminal ones (Submitted, Archived, Reverted) live in '

E         +                     'background task',

E         +                 'definition': 'A Proc is a durable background process SASE records, '

E         +                 "surfaced by `sase proc` and ACE's Procs tab. Historical proc "

E         +     'axe': {
E         +         'max_hook_runners': 3,
E         +         'max_agent_runners': 3,
E         +         'zombie_timeout_seconds': 7200,
E         +         'lumberjack_log_max_bytes': 52428800,
E         +         'lumberjack_log_temp_max_age_seconds': 300,
E         +         'lumberjack_restart_backoff_max_seconds': 60,
E         +         'verbose_lumberjack_diagnostics': False,
E         +         'query': '',
E         +         'chop_script_dirs': [],
E         +         'lumberjacks': {
E         +             'hooks': {
E         +                 'description': 'Fast lane that advances hook, mentor, and workflow lifecycle '
E         +                 'state every few seconds\n'
E         +                 '\n'
E         +                 'Runs every five seconds with a 90-second per-chop timeout so '
E         +                 'completed work is noticed and new work starts\n'
E         +                 'promptly. Put latency-sensitive Patch lifecycle '
E         +                 'reconciliation here; slower remote polling, wait\n'
E         +                 'coordination, and maintenance belong in the other lanes.',
E         +                 'interval': 5,
E         +                 'chop_timeout': '90s',
E         +                 'chops': [
E         +                     {
E         +                         'name': 'hook_checks',
E         +                         'script': 'sase_chop_hook_checks',
E         +                         'description': 'Complete finished hooks and start stale ones, with '
E         +                         'zombie detection\n'
E         +                         '\n'
E         +                         'Scans hook entries on every matching Patch, records '
E         +                         'completed process results, and starts stale hooks\n'
E         +                         'when a runner slot is free. Honors max_hook_runners '
E         +                         'across the tick; stale fix-hook suffixes older than\n'
E         +                         'zombie_timeout_seconds become ZOMBIE, while terminal '
E         +                         'Patches may finish hooks but cannot start new ones.',
E         +                     },
E         +                     {
E         +                         'name': 'mentor_checks',
E         +                         'script': 'sase_chop_mentor_checks',
E         +                         'description': 'Start mentor workflows once all hook prerequisites '
E         +                         'are met\n'
E         +                         '\n'
E         +                         'Reconciles running mentors, stops mentors left behind '
E         +                         'by older commits, adds matching mentor profiles, and\n'
E         +                         'launches ready profiles after their hooks finish. '
E         +                         'Mentor launches share max_agent_runners with other '
E         +                         'agent\n'
E         +                         'workflows, and review-ineligible or terminal Patches '
E         +                         'are skipped.',
E         +                     },
E         +                     {
E         +                         'name': 'workflow_checks',
E         +                         'script': 'sase_chop_workflow_checks',
E         +                         'description': 'Complete finished CRS/fix-hook workflows and start '
E         +                         'stale ones\n'
E         +                         '\n'
E         +                         'Reads workflow state from every matching Patch, '
E         +                         'records results for finished CRS and fix-hook '
E         +                         'agents,\n'
E         +                         'and launches stale workflows. New workflows share '
E         +                         "max_agent_runners and the current tick's "
E         +                         'agent-launch\n'
E         +                         'budget with mentors, so a full runner pool defers '
E         +                         'work instead of queueing it.',
E         +                     },
E         +                     {
E         +                         'name': 'pending_checks_poll',
E         +                         'script': 'sase_chop_pending_checks_poll',
E         +                         'description': 'Poll background is_cl_submitted and critique_comments '
E         +                         'checks for results\n'
E         +                         '\n'
E         +                         'Scans the pending-check directory once per tick, '
E         +                         'applies completed results to matching Patches, and\n'
E         +                         'reaps output files orphaned by killed or crashed '
E         +                         'checks. This chop only consumes background results;\n'
E         +                         'pr_submitted_checks and comment_checks launch the '
E         +                         'remote checks.',
E         +                     },
E         +                     {
E         +                         'name': 'comment_zombie_checks',
E         +                         'script': 'sase_chop_comment_zombie_checks',
E         +                         'description': 'Mark comment threads older than zombie_timeout as '
E         +                         'ZOMBIE\n'
E         +                         '\n'
E         +                         'Examines comment-entry suffix timestamps on matching '
E         +                         'Patches and writes a ZOMBIE suffix when an entry\n'
E         +                         'exceeds zombie_timeout_seconds. It performs no remote '
E         +                         'comment fetch; comment_checks starts those checks '
E         +                         'and\n'
E         +                         'pending_checks_poll applies their results.',
E         +                     },
E         +                     {
E         +                         'name': 'suffix_transforms',
E         +                         'script': 'sase_chop_suffix_transforms',

E         +                 '\n'
E         +                 'The current chop targets only `sase` and is guarded by the '
E         +                 '`toobig-` clan prefix, so a previous split-file swarm\n'
E         +                 'must clear before another scan can propose work.',
E         +                 'interval': 60,

E         +                 'chops': {

E         +             'ci_watch': {
E         +                 'description': 'Watch GitHub Actions health across SASE repos and drive fixes '
E         +                 'or releases\n'
E         +                 '\n'
E         +                 'Runs every five minutes. The single chop owns its own actstat '
E         +                 'config so unrelated orgs are never swept,\n'
E         +                 'classifies high-cadence repos from terminal job evidence '
E         +                 'instead of blanket in-flight state, and files at\n'
E         +                 'most one debounced LaunchApproval gate per tick for a durable '
E         +                 'CI-fix agent the user approves or rejects.\n'
E         +                 'Gates are suppressed while a `ci_fix` hood agent is live, '
E         +                 'while any gate is still pending, or once the same\n'
E         +                 'failing-job fingerprint has already been gated; the gate '
E         +                 "prompt's `%w(runners=0)` delays an approved fixer\n"
E         +                 'until the machine is idle. The chop also guards release '
E         +                 'merges because no SASE repo has branch protection,\n'
E         +                 'publishes a durable current release report, and links release '
E         +                 'notifications directly to that report.',
E         +                 'interval': 300,
E         +                 'chop_timeout': '120s',
E         +                 'chops': {
E         +                     'ci_watch': {
E         +                         'script': 'bugyi_chop_ci_watch',
E         +                         'description': 'Sweep actstat once, file non-overlapping CI-fix '
E         +                         'gates, and merge ready release PRs\n'
E         +                         '\n'
E         +                         'Terminal job failures file one sha-independent, '
E         +                         'deduplicated LaunchApproval gate after the same '
E         +                         'failing\n'
E         +                         'job fingerprint survives the debounce window '
E         +                         '(suppressed while a `ci_fix` hood agent is live, '
E         +                         'while a\n'
E         +                         "gate is already pending, or once that fingerprint's "
E         +                         'episode is already gated; one new gate per tick).\n'
E         +                         "The gate prompt's `%w(runners=0)` keeps an approved "
E         +                         'fixer from competing with interactive work. Green\n'
E         +                         'branches with fully green release PRs merge '
E         +                         'deterministically in dependency order, capped per '
E         +                         'tick.\n'
E         +                         'Repos with no Actions report no_ci, while everything '
E         +                         'unknown fails closed. Every tick publishes the\n'
E         +                         'current release ledger report, and merge or '
E         +                         'blocked-release notifications open that live report '
E         +                         'in ACE.\n'
E         +                         'The whole chop is also inhibited while any SASE agent '
E         +                         'holds a runner slot, so it only sweeps, gates, or\n'
E         +                         'merges on an otherwise idle machine.',

E         +             'tags': 'land_epic',

E         +                 'bead_id': 'word',
E         +             },
E         +             'content': 'You are the land agent for epic bead {{ bead_id }}: verify the '
E         +             'epic is truly complete, integrate it with changes\n'
E         +             'that landed since it started, then close it out.\n'
E         +             '\n'
E         +             '1. Verify. Run `sase bead show {{ bead_id }}` (children, linked '
E         +             "plan file), review the epic bead's own notes, then\n"
E         +             '   run `sase bead show` on every child and review every child '
E         +             'note. Confirm each note was addressed, and read the\n'
E         +             "   actual source code and the epic's commits (bead IDs appear in "
E         +             'commit messages) to confirm the work previous\n'
E         +             '   agents reported complete really is. While reviewing child '
E         +             'beads, collect every `PROPOSED FOLLOW-UP:` note entry.\n'
E         +             '\n'
E         +             '2. Integrate. Changes committed since this epic started could not '
E         +             "integrate with this epic's feature while it was\n"
E         +             '   incomplete. Find them (e.g. `git log` since the first commit '
E         +             "mentioning {{ bead_id }}, excluding the epic's own\n"
E         +             '   commits; in a PR workflow also review commits on the base '
E         +             'branch) and update anything that should now use what\n'
E         +             '   this epic added or that duplicates or conflicts with it. This '
E         +             "integration is part of the epic's work.\n"
E         +             '\n'
E         +             '3. Land. Unresolved issues caused by this epic remain epic work: '
E         +             'plan and finish them before closing. For each\n'
E         +             '   genuinely distinct follow-up that is not caused by the epic, '
E         +             'use `/sase_new_task` with details identifying the\n'
E         +             '   proposing bead; it will corroborate a duplicate, attach a '
E         +             'causally related active-epic issue, or create a sized\n'
E         +             '   task as appropriate. Record every outcome, including why any '
E         +             'proposal was declined, in your close note. Close\n'
E         +             '   the epic with `sase bead close {{ bead_id }} --note "<what you '
E         +             'verified in steps 1-2>"`. AFTER closing, run\n'
E         +             '   `just symvision` if available (epic-symbol whitelist entries '
E         +             'for {{ bead_id }} expire at close) and remove the\n'
E         +             '   stale entries and unused code it reports. Finally, set '
E         +             "`status: done` in the frontmatter of the epic's plan\n"
E         +             '   file (the PLAN path shown by `sase bead show`). If the close '
E         +             'is rejected, the named phases were never\n'
E         +             '   completed: finish or reopen them, or record the outcome '
E         +             'deliberately with\n'
E         +             '   `--force --reason ... --resolution canceled|superseded`. Never '
E         +             'force merely to make the command succeed, and\n'
E         +             '   never use `--force` to advance a successful nested landing.\n'
E         +             '\n'
E         +             'If steps 1-2 uncover remaining work, use your /sase_plan skill to '
E         +             "plan it and complete the skill's tier-aware\n"
E         +             'validate/revalidate/propose loop. Plan only the remaining work. '
E         +             "Do not include this epic's close, symvision pass,\n"
E         +             "or plan-file status update as a child phase; the child epic's "
E         +             '`parent_bead` link is the handoff that lets its land\n'
E         +             'agent resume this interrupted landing after the child lands.\n'
E         +             '\n'
E         +             'After the current epic closes, inspect the linked `parent_bead` '
E         +             'from `sase bead show {{ bead_id }}`. If there is\n'
E         +             'no parent bead, finish normally. If the parent is a phase bead, '
E         +             'verify this child plan completed the work required\n'

E         +             'descendant and linked-plan readiness checks before closing it. '

E         +             'mark its linked plan file done, and then repeat through directly '

E         +             'complete. Stop at the first incomplete or ambiguous parent, '
E         +             'record a note on that parent describing the blocker,\n'

    def test_load_merged_config_caches_plugin_layer(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        """Plugin default configs are loaded once and reused across cache misses."""
        global_dir = tmp_path / "global"
        _write_user_config(global_dir, {"key": "user"})

        real_loader = config_core._load_plugin_configs

            return real_loader()

            patch("sase.config.core.CONFIG_DIR", global_dir),
            patch("sase.config.core.Path.cwd", return_value=tmp_path / "no_local"),
            patch("sase.config.core._load_plugin_configs", counting_loader),
            patch("sase.config.core.time.monotonic", side_effect=lambda: now[0]),

    def _wait_for_new_merged_config(previous: dict) -> dict:
        """Wait for a background token refresh to invalidate *previous*."""
        deadline = time.perf_counter() + 2.0
        while time.perf_counter() < deadline:
            current = load_merged_config()
            if current is not previous:
                return current
            time.sleep(min(0.01, max(0.0, deadline - time.perf_counter())))

    def test_current_config_token_refresh_is_single_flight() -> None:
        """Concurrent expired reads coalesce behind one daemon recompute."""

        release_refresh = threading.Event()
        calls = 0
