[gw9] linux -- Python 3.14.3 /home/bryan/.local/state/sase/workspaces/sase-org/sase/sase_14/.venv/bin/python

    @pytest.mark.asyncio
    async def test_reload_does_not_block_event_loop() -> None:
        """While the disk scan is in flight, other coroutines must still run.
    
        On the old synchronous implementation, `find_all_patches()` runs on
        the event-loop thread — so nothing else (including a navigation
        keypress) can progress until it returns. The async variant pushes the
        call to `asyncio.to_thread`, leaving the loop free.
        """
        disk_cs = [_make_cs("alpha"), _make_cs("beta"), _make_cs("gamma")]
    
        entered = threading.Event()
        release = threading.Event()
    
        def slow_find_all() -> list[MagicMock]:
            entered.set()
            release.wait(timeout=1.0)
            return disk_cs
    
        app = FakeApp([_make_cs("alpha"), _make_cs("beta"), _make_cs("gamma")])
    
        with (
            patch(
                "sase.ace.patch.find_all_patches_cached",
                side_effect=slow_find_all,
            ),
            patch.object(
                PatchMixin,
                "_filter_patches",
                side_effect=lambda _all: disk_cs,
            ),
            patch(
                "sase.core.query_corpus_facade.compile_query_corpus",
                side_effect=_fake_query_corpus,
            ),
        ):
            reload_task = asyncio.create_task(app._reload_and_reposition_async())
            try:
                assert await asyncio.wait_for(asyncio.to_thread(entered.wait), timeout=0.5)
                # Simulate a navigation keypress that fires while the disk scan is
                # still blocked in the worker thread. On the sync implementation
                # this would queue up behind the scan. On the async implementation
                # it runs immediately.
                heartbeat = asyncio.Event()
                asyncio.get_running_loop().call_soon(heartbeat.set)
                await asyncio.wait_for(heartbeat.wait(), timeout=0.05)
                assert not reload_task.done()
                app.current_idx = 2  # user pressed j twice
            finally:
                release.set()
>           await reload_task

           'lumberjacks': {'checks': {'chops': [{'description': 'Raise one human '
                                                                'gate for each '
                                                                'ready or '
                                                                'snoozed task '
                                                                'bead\n'
                                                                '\n'
                                                                'Scans enabled '
                                                                'projects every '
                                                                'five minutes '
                                                                'and gives every '
                                                                'live task bead '
                                                                'exactly one '
                                                                'pending gate: '
                                                                'a\n'
                                                                'TaskTriage gate '
                                                                'while it is '
                                                                'ready, a '
                                                                'BeadSnooze wake '
                                                                'gate while it '
                                                                'is snoozed. '
                                                                'Deterministic '
                                                                'gate\n'
                                                                'generations in '
                                                                'lane state '
                                                                'prevent '
                                                                'duplicate '
                                                                'notifications, '
                                                                'a gate of the '
                                                                'wrong kind is '
                                                                'replaced when '
                                                                'its\n'
                                                                "bead's status "
                                                                'changes, and a '
                                                                "snoozed bead's "
                                                                'notification is '
                                                                're-snoozed to '
                                                                'its wake time '
                                                                'if it ever '
                                                                'drifts.\n'
                                                                'Gates are '
                                                                'canceled when '
                                                                'their beads '
                                                                'leave those '
                                                                'states, while '
                                                                'answered or '
                                                                'missing gates '
                                                                'can be '
                                                                'regenerated\n'
                                                                'safely if work '
                                                                'remains. Gates '
                                                                'stranded by '
                                                                'removed '
                                                                'projects or '

                                                                'without\n'
                                                                'touching '
                                                                'projects that '

                                                                'unreadable. A '
                                                                'ready bead with '
                                                                'a detached '

                                                                'is deferred '
                                                                'instead of '
                                                                're-gated.',
                                                 'name': 'bead_task_triage',

                                                 'timeout': '2m'},

                                      'description': 'Poll slower PR-submission '

                                                     '\n'
                                                     'Runs every five minutes '
                                                     'for checks that can '

                                                     'polling while retaining a '

                                                     'minute-level comments, and '
                                                     'hourly\n'
                                                     'maintenance deliberately '
                                                     'live elsewhere.',
                                      'interval': 300},
                           'ci_watch': {'chop_timeout': '120s',
                                        'chops': {'ci_watch': {'description': 'Sweep '
                                                                              'actstat '
                                                                              'once, '
                                                                              'file '
                                                                              'non-overlapping '
                                                                              'CI-fix '
                                                                              'gates, '

                                                                              'deduplicated '
                                                                              'LaunchApproval '

                                                                              'gate '
                                                                              'is '
                                                                              'already '
                                                                              'pending, '
                                                                              'or '
                                                                              'once '
                                                                              'that '
                                                                              "fingerprint's "
                                                                              'episode '
                                                                              'is '
                                                                              'already '
                                                                              'gated; '
                                                                              'one '
                                                                              'new '
                                                                              'gate '
                                                                              'per '
                                                                              'tick).\n'
                                                                              'The '
                                                                              'gate '
                                                                              "prompt's "
                                                                              '`%w(runners=0)` '
                                                                              'keeps '
                                                                              'an '
                                                                              'approved '
                                                                              'fixer '
                                                                              'from '

                                                                              'with '
                                                                              'interactive '

                                                                              'Green\n'
                                                                              'branches '
                                                                              'with '
                                                                              'fully '
                                                                              'green '
                                                                              'release '
                                                                              'PRs '
                                                                              'merge '
                                                                              'deterministically '
                                                                              'in '
                                                                              'dependency '
                                                                              'order, '
                                                                              'capped '

                                                                              'Repos '
                                                                              'with '
                                                                              'no '

                                                                              'while '

                                                                              'open '

                                                                              'ACE.\n'
                                                                              'The '
                                                                              'whole '
                                                                              'chop '
                                                                              'is '
                                                                              'also '
                                                                              'inhibited '
                                                                              'while '
                                                                              'any '
                                                                              'SASE '
                                                                              'agent '
                                                                              'holds '
                                                                              'a '
                                                                              'runner '
                                                                              'slot, '
                                                                              'so '
                                                                              'it '
                                                                              'only '
                                                                              'sweeps, '
                                                                              'gates, '
                                                                              'or\n'
                                                                              'merges '
                                                                              'on '
                                                                              'an '
                                                                              'otherwise '
                                                                              'idle '
                                                                              'machine.',
                                                               'env': {'ACTSTAT_CONFIG': '/home/bryan/.config/sase/actstat-ci-watch.yml'},
                                                               'inhibit_if': {'agent_runners': {'max': 0}},
                                                               'script': 'bugyi_chop_ci_watch',
                                                               'vars': {'actstat_bin': '/home/bryan/.cargo/bin/actstat',
                                                                        'fix_enabled': True,
                                                                        'gh_bin': 'gh',
                                                                        'max_fix_proposals_per_tick': 1,
                                                                        'max_merges_per_tick': 1,
                                                                        'merge_enabled': True,
                                                                        'merge_order': ['sase-core',
                                                                                        'sase-github',
                                                                                        'sase-telegram',
                                                                                        'sase'],
                                                                        'red_debounce_ticks': 2,

                                                                        'repos': ['sase-org/sase',

                                                                                  'sase-org/sase-telegram',
                                                                                  'sase-org/sase-nvim'],

                                        'description': 'Watch GitHub Actions '
                                                       'health across SASE repos '
                                                       'and drive fixes or '
                                                       'releases\n'
                                                       '\n'
                                                       'Runs every five minutes. '
                                                       'The single chop owns its '
                                                       'own actstat config so '
                                                       'unrelated orgs are never '
                                                       'swept,\n'
                                                       'classifies high-cadence '
                                                       'repos from terminal job '
                                                       'evidence instead of '
                                                       'blanket in-flight state, '
                                                       'and files at\n'
                                                       'most one debounced '
                                                       'LaunchApproval gate per '
                                                       'tick for a durable '
                                                       'CI-fix agent the user '
                                                       'approves or rejects.\n'
                                                       'Gates are suppressed '
                                                       'while a `ci_fix` hood '
                                                       'agent is live, while any '
                                                       'gate is still pending, '
                                                       'or once the same\n'
                                                       'failing-job fingerprint '
                                                       'has already been gated; '
                                                       "the gate prompt's "
                                                       '`%w(runners=0)` delays '
                                                       'an approved fixer\n'
                                                       'until the machine is '
                                                       'idle. The chop also '
                                                       'guards release merges '
                                                       'because no SASE repo has '
                                                       'branch protection,\n'
                                                       'publishes a durable '
                                                       'current release report, '
                                                       'and links release '
                                                       'notifications directly '
                                                       'to that report.',
                                        'interval': 300},
                           'comments': {'chops': [{'description': 'Start '
                                                                  'background '
                                                                  'critique_comments '
                                                                  'checks for '
                                                                  'all mailed '

                                                                  '\n'
                                                                  'Applies the '
                                                                  'axe query and '
                                                                  'starts '
                                                                  'non-blocking '
                                                                  'critique_comments '
                                                                  'checks for '
                                                                  'mailed '
                                                                  'Patches that '
                                                                  'have an\n'
                                                                  'available '
                                                                  'workspace, '
                                                                  'then records '
                                                                  'a '
                                                                  'comment-cycle '
                                                                  'summary. The '
                                                                  "lumberjack's "
                                                                  'one-minute '
                                                                  'interval is '
                                                                  'the\n'
                                                                  'polling '
                                                                  'throttle; '

                                                                  'background '

                                        'description': 'Start background '
                                                       'critique-comment checks '
                                                       'for mailed PRs every '
                                                       'minute\n'
                                                       '\n'
                                                       'Runs every minute so '
                                                       'reviewer feedback '
                                                       'reaches active Patches '
                                                       'promptly without polling '
                                                       'on every hooks tick.\n'
                                                       'Only remote '
                                                       'comment-check launches '
                                                       'belong here; pending '
                                                       'result collection and '
                                                       'zombie marking remain in '
                                                       'the\n'
                                                       'faster hooks lane.',
                                        'interval': 60},
                           'external_mirror': {'chop_timeout': '5m',
                                               'chops': [{'description': 'Mirror '
                                                                         'every '
                                                                         'external '
                                                                         'tracker '
                                                                         'issue '
                                                                         'into a '
                                                                         'task '
                                                                         'bead\n'
                                                                         '\n'
                                                                         'Expands '
                                                                         'to one '
                                                                         'instance '
                                                                         'per '
                                                                         'enabled '
                                                                         'project '
                                                                         'and '
                                                                         'diffs '
                                                                         'that '
                                                                         "project's "
                                                                         'tracker '
                                                                         'against '
                                                                         'local '
                                                                         'beads '
                                                                         'on\n'
                                                                         'external_ref, '
                                                                         'creating '
                                                                         'small '
                                                                         'open '
                                                                         'task '
                                                                         'beads '
                                                                         'for '
                                                                         'uncovered '
                                                                         'issues. '
                                                                         'Upstream '
                                                                         'closes '
                                                                         'and '
                                                                         'reopens '
                                                                         'update\n'
                                                                         'mirrored '
                                                                         'beads '

                                                                         'only. '

                                                                         'tracker '
                                                                         'from '
                                                                         'stalling '
                                                                         'the '

                                                                         'and '

                                                                         'a '

                                                                         'full '

                                                                         'scan.\n'

                                                                         'are\n'
                                                                         'adopted '
                                                                         'as '

                                                                       'vcs': ['git',

                                               'interval': 900},
                           'hooks': {'chop_timeout': '90s',

                           'refresh_docs': {'chops': {'refresh_docs': {'description': 'Refresh '

                                                           '\n'
                                                           'Runs every 60 '
                                                           'seconds so each '

                                                           'let '

                                                           'documentation has '

                                                           'enough to refresh. '

                                            'interval': 60,

                           'run_every': {'chops': {'toobig_split': {'description': 'Propose '

                                                                                 'source': 'projects'},
                                                                    'inhibit_if': {'agent_clan': {'name_prefix': 'toobig-'}},

                                                                    'vars': {'limits': ['1000',

                                         'description': 'Propose scheduled '
                                                        'maintenance for '
                                                        'configured projects '
                                                        'once a minute\n'
                                                        '\n'
                                                        'Runs every 60 seconds '
                                                        'so low-cost maintenance '
                                                        'checks can notice stale '
                                                        'conditions without '
                                                        'waiting for a slower\n'
                                                        'audit lane. Use this '
                                                        'lane for chores '
                                                        'controlled mainly by '
                                                        '`run_every` and AXE '
                                                        'inhibit guards, not '
                                                        'for\n'
                                                        'commit-threshold '
                                                        'reviews or '
                                                        'documentation '
                                                        'refreshes.\n'
                                                        '\n'
                                                        'The current chop '
                                                        'targets only `sase` and '
                                                        'is guarded by the '
                                                        '`toobig-` clan prefix, '
                                                        'so a previous '
                                                        'split-file swarm\n'
                                                        'must clear before '
                                                        'another scan can '
                                                        'propose work.',
                                         'interval': 60,
                                         'wait_runners': 3},
                           'telegram': {'chops': {'tg_inbound': {'description': 'Process '

                                                                                'Polls '

                                                                                'API '
                                                                                'for '
                                                                                'callbacks, '
                                                                                'slash '
                                                                                'commands, '

                                                                                'text, '

                                                                                'writes '
                                                                                'the '
                                                                                'matching '

                                                                                'request '
                                                                                'for '
                                                                                'core '
                                                                                'workflows '
                                                                                'to '
                                                                                'consume.\n'
                                                                                '\n'
                                                                                'This '
                                                                                'chop '
                                                                                'runs '
                                                                                'on '
                                                                                'every '
                                                                                'five-second '
                                                                                'tick '
                                                                                'so '
                                                                                'approvals '

                                                                                'questions '
                                                                                'resolve '
                                                                                'quickly. '
                                                                                'It '
                                                                                'uses '
                                                                                'the\n'
                                                                                'lumberjack-level '
                                                                                'bot '
                                                                                'environment; '
                                                                                'Telegram '
                                                                                'launch-disabling '
                                                                                'policy '
                                                                                'can '
                                                                                'still '
                                                                                'block '
                                                                                'free-form '
                                                                                'agent '
                                                                                'starts\n'
                                                                                'without '
                                                                                'stopping '
                                                                                'callbacks '
                                                                                'or '
                                                                                'feedback '
                                                                                'responses.',
                                                                 'script': 'sase_chop_tg_inbound'},
                                                  'tg_outbound': {'description': 'Send '
                                                                                 'unread '
                                                                                 'notifications '
                                                                                 'to '
                                                                                 'Telegram\n'
                                                                                 '\n'
                                                                                 'Reads '
                                                                                 'the '
                                                                                 'same '
                                                                                 'SASE '
                                                                                 'notification '
                                                                                 'store '
                                                                                 'used '
                                                                                 'by '
                                                                                 'ACE, '
                                                                                 'formats '
                                                                                 'unread '
                                                                                 'non-silent '
                                                                                 'notifications '
                                                                                 'for '
                                                                                 'Telegram,\n'
                                                                                 'and '
                                                                                 'sends '
                                                                                 'messages, '
                                                                                 'PDFs, '
                                                                                 'or '
                                                                                 'generated '
                                                                                 'images '
                                                                                 'to '
                                                                                 'the '
                                                                                 'configured '
                                                                                 'chat.\n'
                                                                                 '\n'
                                                                                 'The '
                                                                                 'fast '
                                                                                 'cadence '
                                                                                 'keeps '
                                                                                 'approval '
                                                                                 'requests '
                                                                                 'and '
                                                                                 'completion '
                                                                                 'notices '
                                                                                 'visible '
                                                                                 'without '
                                                                                 'a '
                                                                                 'daemon. '
                                                                                 'Delivery '
                                                                                 'state '
                                                                                 'and\n'
                                                                                 'rate-limit '
                                                                                 'checks '
                                                                                 'stay '
                                                                                 'in '
                                                                                 'the '
                                                                                 'Telegram '
                                                                                 'plugin, '
                                                                                 'so '
                                                                                 'repeated '
                                                                                 'AXE '
                                                                                 'ticks '
                                                                                 'do '
                                                                                 'not '
                                                                                 'resend '
                                                                                 'handled '
                                                                                 'notifications.',
                                                                  'script': 'sase_chop_tg_outbound'}},
                                        'description': 'Fast lane that relays '
                                                       'Telegram messages and '
                                                       'notification delivery '
                                                       'every few seconds\n'
                                                       '\n'
                                                       'Runs every five seconds '
                                                       'because chat buttons, '
                                                       'slash commands, and '
                                                       'outbound notifications '
                                                       'are useful only when\n'
                                                       'they feel '
                                                       'near-real-time. Keep '
                                                       'Telegram transport chops '
                                                       'in this lane; broader '
                                                       'notification maintenance '
                                                       'belongs\n'
                                                       'elsewhere so bot network '
                                                       'calls do not delay '
                                                       'unrelated work.\n'
                                                       '\n'
                                                       'The lumberjack supplies '
                                                       'the bot username, chat '
                                                       'id, and token '
                                                       'environment to both '
                                                       'chops. It intentionally '
                                                       'avoids\n'
                                                       '`run_every` delays so '
                                                       'inbound decisions and '
                                                       'outbound '
                                                       'acknowledgements are '
                                                       'checked on every tick.',
                                        'env': {'SASE_TELEGRAM_BOT_CHAT_ID': '8990449281',
                                                'SASE_TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN': {'pass': 'telegram_sase_bot_token'},
                                                'SASE_TELEGRAM_BOT_USERNAME': 'sase_athena_bot'},
                                        'interval': 5},
                           'waits': {'chops': [{'description': 'Acquire missing '
                                                               'bead claims for '
                                                               'live pre-launch '
                                                               'agents and '
                                                               'release claims '
                                                               'held by dead '
                                                               'ones\n'
                                                               '\n'
                                                               'Scans pre-launch '
                                                               'agent artifacts, '
                                                               'backfills a '
                                                               'missing claim '
                                                               'for a live '
                                                               'waiting agent, '
                                                               'and releases a\n'
                                                               'claimed bead '
                                                               'when its '
                                                               'unpromoted owner '
                                                               'has died. '
                                                               'Reconciled dead '
                                                               'records are '
                                                               'tombstoned so '
                                                               'later ticks '
                                                               'avoid\n'
                                                               'reopening their '
                                                               'stores, while a '
                                                               'failed project '
                                                               'read is retried '
                                                               'safely.',
                                                'name': 'bead_claim_checks',
                                                'script': 'sase_chop_bead_claim_checks'},
                                               {'description': 'Integrate the '
                                                               'canonical bead '
                                                               'store for '
                                                               'projects with '
                                                               'agents waiting '
                                                               'on beads\n'
                                                               '\n'
                                                               'Finds projects '
                                                               'with a live '
                                                               'agent waiting on '
                                                               'bead completion '
                                                               'and refreshes '
                                                               'each canonical '
                                                               'store at most\n'
                                                               'every 30 '
                                                               'seconds. The '
                                                               'two-minute '
                                                               'timeout is '
                                                               'protected by '
                                                               'bounded '
                                                               'per-project lock '
                                                               'waits, a '
                                                               'whole-pass work\n'
                                                               'budget, and '
                                                               'persistent '
                                                               'exponential '
                                                               'backoff so one '
                                                               'contended store '
                                                               'cannot stall all '
                                                               'waiters.',
                                                'name': 'bead_store_refresh',
                                                'run_every': '30s',
                                                'script': 'sase_chop_bead_store_refresh',
                                                'timeout': '2m'},
                                               {'description': 'Flush planner '
                                                               'completion '
                                                               'notifications '
                                                               'orphaned by an '
                                                               'unsettled epic '
                                                               'launch\n'
                                                               '\n'
                                                               'Preserves '
                                                               'deferrals while '
                                                               'a matching '
                                                               'detached '
                                                               'epic-launch task '
                                                               'is active, '
                                                               'flushes unowned '
                                                               'deferrals after\n'
                                                               'a 90-second '
                                                               'grace period '
                                                               'with a resume '
                                                               'command, and '
                                                               'reaps unclaimed '
                                                               'settle markers '
                                                               'after one hour.',
                                                'name': 'epic_launch_flush',
                                                'run_every': '30s',
                                                'script': 'sase_chop_epic_launch_flush'},
                                               {'description': 'Fetch and '
                                                               'fast-forward '
                                                               'opted-in primary '
                                                               'sidecar clones '
                                                               '(plans, beads, '
                                                               'research, '
                                                               'custom)\n'
                                                               '\n'
                                                               'Scans every '
                                                               'enabled '
                                                               "project's "
                                                               'auto_sync '
                                                               'sidecar roles, '
                                                               'syncing a role '
                                                               'immediately when '
                                                               'a publisher left '
                                                               'a\n'
                                                               'pending hint and '
                                                               'otherwise '
                                                               'backstopping it '
                                                               'at most every '
                                                               'five minutes. '
                                                               'Only a clean, '
                                                               'attached, '
                                                               'non-diverged\n'
                                                               'clone with a '
                                                               'matching remote '
                                                               'is fetched and '
                                                               'fast-forwarded; '
                                                               'dirty, detached, '
                                                               'diverged, '
                                                               'mismatched, '
                                                               'missing,\n'
                                                               'or busy clones '
                                                               'are left '
                                                               'untouched and '
                                                               'reported. '
                                                               'Bounded work '
                                                               'budget and '
                                                               'persistent '
                                                               'per-role backoff '
                                                               'keep one\n'
                                                               'unhealthy clone '
                                                               'from stalling '
                                                               'the rest.',
                                                'name': 'sidecar_auto_sync',
                                                'run_every': '30s',
                                                'script': 'sase_chop_sidecar_auto_sync',
                                                'timeout': '2m'},
                                               {'description': 'Resolve agent '
                                                               'wait '
                                                               'dependencies and '
                                                               'write ready.json '
                                                               'when satisfied\n'
                                                               '\n'
                                                               'Scans waiting '
                                                               'markers across '
                                                               'projects and '
                                                               'resolves '
                                                               'named-agent, '
                                                               'artifact, and '
                                                               'closed-bead '
                                                               'dependencies '
                                                               'from\n'
                                                               'shared agent '
                                                               'metadata and '
                                                               'canonical bead '
                                                               'state. It writes '
                                                               'ready.json only '
                                                               'after every '
                                                               'dependency is\n'
                                                               'satisfied; '
                                                               'invalid or '
                                                               'already-ready '
                                                               'markers are '
                                                               'skipped without '
                                                               'blocking other '
                                                               'agents.',
                                                'name': 'wait_checks',
                                                'script': 'sase_chop_wait_checks'}],
                                     'description': 'Resolve agent wait '
                                                    'dependencies and keep bead '
                                                    'claims and stores in sync\n'
                                                    '\n'
                                                    'Runs every ten seconds so '
                                                    'waiting agents resume '
                                                    'promptly and short-lived '
                                                    'bead claims are reconciled '
                                                    'quickly.\n'
                                                    'Put agent dependency, '
                                                    'bead-claim, and bead-store '
                                                    'coordination here; Patch '
                                                    'lifecycle checks and '
                                                    'general\n'
                                                    'cleanup belong in their '
                                                    'dedicated lanes.',
                                     'interval': 10}},
           'max_agent_runners': 3,
           'max_hook_runners': 3,
           'query': '',
           'verbose_lumberjack_diagnostics': False,
           'zombie_timeout_seconds': 7200},
   'bead': {'big_epic_phase_threshold': 5, 'push_after_commit': True},
   'chat_install': {'restart_attempts': 3, 'timeout_seconds': 900},
   'commit': {'finalizer': {'enabled': True, 'max_passes': 2},
              'message': {'allowed_types': ['build',
                                            'chore',

                                            'revert',
                                            'style',
                                            'test'],
                          'require_conventional_subject': True}},
   'commit_hooks': {'after': '', 'before': 'just fix'},
   'default_linked_repos': True,

                                                     'states': [],
                                                     'title_globs': []}}},
   'file_hooks': [{'command': 'bob highlights create --include-id',
                   'use': 'research-highlights'}],
   'github_orgs': ['bbugyi200', 'sase-org', 'bobs-org'],

                                                 'research_a': {'bucket': 'researchers',
                                                                'description': 'Primary '
                                                                               'independent '
                                                                               'research-swarm '
                                                                               'researcher.',
                                                                'model': 'codex/gpt-5.6-sol'},
                                                 'research_b': {'bucket': 'researchers',
                                                                'description': 'Assisting '
                                                                               '(second-opinion) '
                                                                               'research-swarm '
                                                                               'agents.',
                                                                'model': 'claude/opus'},
                                                 'research_lead': {'bucket': 'researchers',
                                                                   'description': 'Final '
                                                                                  'research-swarm '
                                                                                  'lead '
                                                                                  'and '
                                                                                  'consolidator.',
                                                                   'model': '@smartest'}}},
                    'provider': 'codex',
                    'retry': {'claude': {'error_patterns': ['API Error: 500',
                                                            'API Error: 529',
                                                            'Internal server '
                                                            'error',
                                                            'overloaded_error'],
                                         'fallback_model': 'sonnet',
                                         'max_retries': 3,
                                         'wait_times': [60, 300, 1800]},
                              'codex': {'error_patterns': ['exceeded retry limit',
                                                           '429 Too Many '

                                        'max_retries': 3,
                                        'wait_times': [60, 300, 1800]},
                              'sase': {'error_patterns': ['uses a format this '
